PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain travel_planning)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    city day count
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?c - city ?d - day)
    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)
    (city_count ?c - city ?v - count)
    (next_count ?v1 ?v2 - count)
    (direct_flight ?c1 ?c2 - city)
    (is_meeting_window ?d - day)
    (met_friend)
    (trip_started)
    (trip_concluded)
  )

  ;; Start the trip at a chosen city on day 1.
  (:action start_trip
    :parameters (?c - city)
    :precondition (not (trip_started))
    :effect (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c d01)
    )
  )

  ;; Spend the current day in the same city and move to the next day.
  (:action stay
    :parameters (?c - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?count_curr - count ?count_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (city_count ?c ?count_curr)
      (next_count ?count_curr ?count_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c ?d_curr))
      (at ?c ?d_next)
      (not (city_count ?c ?count_curr))
      (city_count ?c ?count_next)
    )
  )

  ;; Fly between cities. A flight day counts toward the stay duration of both the origin and destination cities.
  (:action fly
    :parameters (?c_from - city ?c_to - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?cf_curr - count ?cf_next - count ?ct_curr - count ?ct_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c_from ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (direct_flight ?c_from ?c_to)
      (city_count ?c_from ?cf_curr)
      (next_count ?cf_curr ?cf_next)
      (city_count ?c_to ?ct_curr)
      (next_count ?ct_curr ?ct_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c_from ?d_curr))
      (at ?c_to ?d_next)
      (not (city_count ?c_from ?cf_curr))
      (city_count ?c_from ?cf_next)
      (not (city_count ?c_to ?ct_curr))
      (city_count ?c_to ?ct_next)
    )
  )

  ;; Meet a friend in Florence if the agent is present during the window.
  (:action meet_friend
    :parameters (?d - day)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at florence ?d)
      (is_meeting_window ?d)
    )
    :effect (met_friend)
  )

  ;; Conclude the trip on day 14 to count the final day of stay.
  (:action conclude_trip
    :parameters (?c - city ?curr_count - count ?next_count - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (not (trip_concluded))
      (at ?c d14)
      (city_count ?c ?curr_count)
      (next_count ?curr_count ?next_count)
    )
    :effect (and
      (trip_concluded)
      (not (city_count ?c ?curr_count))
      (city_count ?c ?next_count)
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem travel_14_days)
  (:domain travel_planning)
  (:objects
    helsinki barcelona florence - city
    d01 d02 d03 d04 d05 d06 d07 d08 d09 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day
    c0 c1 c2 c3 c4 c5 c6 - count
  )

  (:init
    ;; Day sequence
    (next_day d01 d02)
    (next_day d02 d03)
    (next_day d03 d04)
    (next_day d04 d05)
    (next_day d05 d06)
    (next_day d06 d07)
    (next_day d07 d08)
    (next_day d08 d09)
    (next_day d09 d10)
    (next_day d10 d11)
    (next_day d11 d12)
    (next_day d12 d13)
    (next_day d13 d14)

    ;; Count sequence
    (next_count c0 c1)
    (next_count c1 c2)
    (next_count c2 c3)
    (next_count c3 c4)
    (next_count c4 c5)
    (next_count c5 c6)

    ;; City stay initialization
    (city_count helsinki c0)
    (city_count barcelona c0)
    (city_count florence c0)

    ;; Flight connectivity
    (direct_flight barcelona florence)
    (direct_flight florence barcelona)
    (direct_flight helsinki barcelona)
    (direct_flight barcelona helsinki)

    ;; Meeting window in Florence (Day 9 to Day 14)
    (is_meeting_window d09)
    (is_meeting_window d10)
    (is_meeting_window d11)
    (is_meeting_window d12)
    (is_meeting_window d13)
    (is_meeting_window d14)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (trip_concluded)
      (met_friend)
      (city_count helsinki c5)
      (city_count barcelona c5)
      (city_count florence c6)
    )
  )
)
</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain travel_planning)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    city day count
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?c - city ?d - day)
    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)
    (city_count ?c - city ?v - count)
    (next_count ?v1 ?v2 - count)
    (direct_flight ?c1 ?c2 - city)
    (is_meeting_window ?d - day)
    (met_friend)
    (trip_started)
    (trip_concluded)
  )

  ;; Start the trip at a chosen city on day 1.
  (:action start_trip
    :parameters (?c - city)
    :precondition (not (trip_started))
    :effect (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c d01)
    )
  )

  ;; Spend the current day in the same city and move to the next day.
  (:action stay
    :parameters (?c - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?count_curr - count ?count_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (city_count ?c ?count_curr)
      (next_count ?count_curr ?count_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c ?d_curr))
      (at ?c ?d_next)
      (not (city_count ?c ?count_curr))
      (city_count ?c ?count_next)
    )
  )

  ;; Fly between cities. A flight day counts toward the stay duration of both the origin and destination cities.
  (:action fly
    :parameters (?c_from - city ?c_to - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?cf_curr - count ?cf_next - count ?ct_curr - count ?ct_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at ?c_from ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (direct_flight ?c_from ?c_to)
      (city_count ?c_from ?cf_curr)
      (next_count ?cf_curr ?cf_next)
      (city_count ?c_to ?ct_curr)
      (next_count ?ct_curr ?ct_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at ?c_from ?d_curr))
      (at ?c_to ?d_next)
      (not (city_count ?c_from ?cf_curr))
      (city_count ?c_from ?cf_next)
      (not (city_count ?c_to ?ct_curr))
      (city_count ?c_to ?ct_next)

  )

  ;; Meet a friend in Florence if the agent is present during the window.
  (:action meet_friend
    :parameters (?d - day)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (at florence ?d)
      (is_meeting_window ?d)
    )
    :effect (met_friend)
  )

  ;; Conclude the trip on day 14 to count the final day of stay.
  (:action conclude_trip
    :parameters (?c - city ?curr_count - count ?next_count - count)
    :precondition (and
      (trip_started)
      (not (trip_concluded))
      (at ?c d14)
      (city_count ?c ?curr_count)
      (next_count ?curr_count ?next_count)
    )
    :effect (and
      (trip_concluded)
      (not (city_count ?c ?curr_count))
      (city_count ?c ?next_count)
    )
  )
)

PROBLEM

(define (problem travel_14_days)
  (:domain travel_planning)
  (:objects
    helsinki barcelona florence - city
    d01 d02 d03 d04 d05 d06 d07 d08 d09 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day
    c0 c1 c2 c3 c4 c5 c6 - count
  )

  (:init
    ;; Day sequence
    (next_day d01 d02)
    (next_day d02 d03)
    (next_day d03 d04)
    (next_day d04 d05)
    (next_day d05 d06)
    (next_day d06 d07)
    (next_day d07 d08)
    (next_day d08 d09)
    (next_day d09 d10)
    (next_day d10 d11)
    (next_day d11 d12)
    (next_day d12 d13)
    (next_day d13 d14)

    ;; Count sequence
    (next_count c0 c1)
    (next_count c1 c2)
    (next_count c2 c3)
    (next_count c3 c4)
    (next_count c4 c5)
    (next_count c5 c6)

    ;; City stay initialization
    (city_count helsinki c0)
    (city_count barcelona c0)
    (city_count florence c0)

    ;; Flight connectivity
    (direct_flight barcelona florence)
    (direct_flight florence barcelona)
    (direct_flight helsinki barcelona)
    (direct_flight barcelona helsinki)

    ;; Meeting window in Florence (Day 9 to Day 14)
    (is_meeting_window d09)
    (is_meeting_window d10)
    (is_meeting_window d11)
    (is_meeting_window d12)
    (is_meeting_window d13)
    (is_meeting_window d14)
  )

  (:goal
    (and
      (trip_concluded)
      (met_friend)
      (city_count helsinki c5)
      (city_count barcelona c5)
      (city_count florence c6)
    )
  )
)

    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem travel_14_days)\n  (:domain travel_planning)\n  (:objects\n    helsinki barcelona florence - city\n    d01 d02 d03 d04 d05 d06 d07 d08 d09 d10 d11 d12 d13 d14 - day\n    c0 c1 c2 c3 c4 c5 c6 - count\n  )\n\n  (:init\n    ;; Day sequence\n    (next_day d01 d02)\n    (next_day d02 d03)\n    (next_day d03 d04)\n    (next_day d04 d05)\n    (next_day d05 d06)\n    (next_day d06 d07)\n    (next_day d07 d08)\n    (next_day d08 d09)\n    (next_day d09 d10)\n    (next_day d10 d11)\n    (next_day d11 d12)\n    (next_day d12 d13)\n    (next_day d13 d14)\n\n    ;; Count sequence\n    (next_count c0 c1)\n    (next_count c1 c2)\n    (next_count c2 c3)\n    (next_count c3 c4)\n    (next_count c4 c5)\n    (next_count c5 c6)\n\n    ;; City stay initialization\n    (city_count helsinki c0)\n    (city_count barcelona c0)\n    (city_count florence c0)\n\n    ;; Flight connectivity\n    (direct_flight barcelona florence)\n    (direct_flight florence barcelona)\n    (direct_flight helsinki barcelona)\n    (direct_flight barcelona helsinki)\n\n    ;; Meeting window in Florence (Day 9 to Day 14)\n    (is_meeting_window d09)\n    (is_meeting_window d10)\n    (is_meeting_window d11)\n    (is_meeting_window d12)\n    (is_meeting_window d13)\n    (is_meeting_window d14)\n  )\n\n  (:goal\n    (and\n      (trip_concluded)\n      (met_friend)\n      (city_count helsinki c5)\n      (city_count barcelona c5)\n      (city_count florence c6)\n    )\n  )\n)",

    Spend your second day in Helsinki. (Helsinki count: 2)

4.  **Day 3: Stay in Helsinki**
    Spend your third day in Helsinki. (Helsinki count: 3)

5.  **Day 4: Stay in Helsinki**
    Spend your fourth day in Helsinki. (Helsinki count: 4)

6.  **Day 5: Travel to Barcelona**
    Spend Day 5 in Helsinki, then take a direct flight to Barcelona, arriving on Day 6. (Helsinki total: 5 days; Barcelona count: 1)

7.  **Day 6: Stay in Barcelona**
    Spend Day 6 in Barcelona. (Barcelona count: 2)

8.  **Day 7: Stay in Barcelona**
    Spend Day 7 in Barcelona. (Barcelona count: 3)

9.  **Day 8: Stay in Barcelona**
    Spend Day 8 in Barcelona. (Barcelona count: 4)

10. **Day 9: Travel to Florence**
    Spend Day 9 in Barcelona, then take a direct flight to Florence, arriving on Day 10. (Barcelona total: 5 days; Florence count: 1)

11. **Day 10: Meet Friend in Florence**
    Upon your arrival in Florence on Day 10, meet your friend as planned. This falls within the required window of Day 9 to Day 14.

12. **Day 10: Stay in Florence**
    Spend Day 10 in Florence. (Florence count: 2)

13. **Day 11: Stay in Florence**
    Spend Day 11 in Florence. (Florence count: 3)

14. **Day 12: Stay in Florence**
    Spend Day 12 in Florence. (Florence count: 4)

15. **Day 13: Stay in Florence**
    Spend Day 13 in Florence. (Florence count: 5)

16. **Day 14: Conclude Trip in Florence**
    Spend your final day (Day 14) in Florence. This brings your total stay in Florence to 6 days. Conclude your 14-day European trip here. (Florence total: 6 days)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_3
------------------------------
This 11-day trip plan covers Reykjavik, Vienna, and Venice, fulfilling all duration requirements and the wedding attendance constraint in Venice.

**Itinerary:**

1.  **Day 1: Start Trip in Reykjavik**
    Begin your journey by arriving in Reykjavik on Day 1.

2.  **Day 1 to Day 2: Reykjavik**
    Spend the duration of Day 1 exploring Reykjavik. By the end of this period, you will have completed 1 of your 2 required days here.

3.  **Day 2 to Day 3: Fly from Reykjavik to Vienna**
    On Day 2, take a direct flight from Reykjavik to Vienna. This travel day counts as the 2nd day for Reykjavik and the 1st day for Vienna.

4.  **Day 3 to Day 4: Vienna**
    Spend Day 3 in Vienna, bringing your total visit duration in the city to 2 days.

5.  **Day 4 to Day 5: Vienna**
    Spend Day 4 in Vienna, bringing your total visit duration in the city to 3 days.

6.  **Day 5 to Day 6: Vienna**
    Spend Day 5 in Vienna, bringing your total visit duration in the city to 4 days.

7.  **Day 6 to Day 7: Vienna**
    Spend Day 6 in Vienna, bringing your total visit duration in the city to 5 days.

8.  **Day 7 to Day 8: Vienna**
    Spend Day 7 in Vienna, bringing your total visit duration in the city to 6 days.

9.  **Day 8 to Day 9: Fly from Vienna to Venice**
    On Day 8, take a direct flight from Vienna to Venice. This travel day counts as your 7th day in Vienna (completing that requirement) and your 1st day in Venice. This also marks the beginning of your wedding attendance in Venice.

10. **Day 9 to Day 10: Venice**
    Spend Day 9 in Venice. This is your 2nd day in the city and the 2nd day of the wedding ceremony.

11. **Day 10 to Day 11: Venice**
    Spend Day 10 in Venice. This is your 3rd day in the city and the 3rd day of the wedding ceremony.

12. **Day 11: Finish Trip in Venice**
    Spend Day 11 in Venice to complete your 4th day in the city and the final day of the wedding. Your 11-day trip concludes here.

To successfully complete your 12-day trip across Seville, Munich, and Tallinn, follow this sequence of actions:

1.  **Day 1:** Start your journey by arriving in Seville.
2.  **Day 1:** Spend your first full day exploring the city of Seville.
3.  **Day 2:** Continue your stay in Seville for a second day.
4.  **Day 3:** Continue your stay in Seville for a third day.
5.  **Day 4:** Continue your stay in Seville for a fourth day.
6.  **Day 5:** Continue your stay in Seville for a fifth day.
7.  **Day 6:** Continue your stay in Seville for a sixth day.
8.  **Day 7:** Travel from Seville to Munich by taking a direct flight. This transition counts as your 7th day in Seville and your 1st day in Munich.
9.  **Day 8:** Spend your second day in Munich.
10. **Day 9:** Spend your third day in Munich.
11. **Day 10:** Spend your fourth day in Munich.
12. **Day 11:** Take a direct flight from Munich to Tallinn. This counts as your 5th day in Munich and your 1st day in Tallinn (fulfilling the requirement to be in Tallinn on Day 11).
13. **Day 12:** Spend the day in Tallinn. This marks your 2nd day in Tallinn (fulfilling the requirement to be in Tallinn on Day 12).

By the end of Day 12, you will have completed your 12-day itinerary, spending exactly 7 days in Seville, 5 days in Munich, and 2 days in Tallinn.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 16 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.

*   **Total Duration:** 16 Days.
*   **Porto:** 7 days (6 stay days + 1 flight day).
*   **Oslo:** 6 days (1 flight day + 4 stay days + 1 flight day).
*   **Dubrovnik:** 5 days (1 flight day + 4 stay days).
*   **Conference Attendance:** Successfully attended in Dubrovnik on Day 12 and Day 16.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2
------------------------------
This 8-day trip plan covers visits to Berlin, Munich, and Dubrovnik, ensuring all conference commitments and travel constraints are met.

*   **Day 1: Berlin**
    Remain in Berlin for the full day. This allows you to attend the required conference sessions on Day 1.
    *(Berlin visit count: 1)*

*   **Day 2: Berlin**
    Spend the entire day in Berlin.
    *(Berlin visit count: 2)*

*   **Day 3: Berlin**
    Spend the entire day in Berlin.
    *(Berlin visit count: 3)*

*   **Day 4: Berlin to Munich**
    Attend the conference in Berlin during the day, then take a direct flight to Munich. Per the travel rules, this day counts as a visit for both cities.
    *(Berlin visit count: 4, Munich visit count: 1)*

*   **Day 5: Munich**
    Spend the entire day in Munich.
    *(Munich visit count: 2)*

*   **Day 6: Munich to Dubrovnik**
    Spend the day in Munich, then take a direct flight to Dubrovnik. This day counts as a visit for both cities.
    *(Munich visit count: 3, Dubrovnik visit count: 1)*

*   **Day 7: Dubrovnik**
    Spend the entire day in Dubrovnik.
    *(Dubrovnik visit count: 2)*

*   **Day 8: Dubrovnik**
    Spend your final day in Dubrovnik.
    *(Dubrovnik visit count: 3)*

**Trip Summary:**
*   **Berlin:** 4 days (Days 1, 2, 3, and 4)
*   **Munich:** 3 days (Days 4, 5, and 6)
*   **Dubrovnik:** 3 days (Days 6, 7, and 8)
*   **Total Duration:** 8 Days
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 11 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Venice for 6 days. You would like to visit Dubrovnik for 4 days. You would like to visit Istanbul for 3 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
from Dubrovnik to Istanbul, Istanbul and Venice.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 11 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 11 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Venice for 6 days. You would like to visit Dubrovnik for 4 days. You would like to visit Istanbul for 3 days.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
This 16-day trip plan outlines your journey through Athens, Zurich, and Krakow, ensuring you meet all your goals, including visiting relatives in Athens and using direct flights between cities.

1.  **Day 1 to Day 2:** Spend your first day in Athens, continuing your stay into the second day. (Athens visit count: 2)
2.  **Day 2 to Day 3:** Continue your stay in Athens. (Athens visit count: 3)
3.  **Day 3 to Day 4:** Continue your stay in Athens. (Athens visit count: 4)
4.  **Day 4 to Day 5:** Continue your stay in Athens. (Athens visit count: 5)
5.  **Day 5 to Day 6:** Continue your stay in Athens. (Athens visit count: 6)
6.  **Day 6 to Day 7:** Spend your final full day in Athens, completing your 7-day visit with your relatives. (Athens visit count: 7)
7.  **Day 7:** Take a direct flight from Athens to Zurich. (Zurich visit count: 1)
8.  **Day 7 to Day 8:** Begin your stay in Zurich, continuing into the eighth day. (Zurich visit count: 2)
9.  **Day 8 to Day 9:** Continue your stay in Zurich. (Zurich visit count: 3)
10. **Day 9 to Day 10:** Continue your stay in Zurich. (Zurich visit count: 4)
11. **Day 10 to Day 11:** Spend your final day in Zurich, completing your 5-day visit. (Zurich visit count: 5)
12. **Day 11:** Take a direct flight from Zurich to Krakow. (Krakow visit count: 1)
13. **Day 11 to Day 12:** Begin your stay in Krakow, continuing into the twelfth day. (Krakow visit count: 2)
14. **Day 12 to Day 13:** Continue your stay in Krakow. (Krakow visit count: 3)
15. **Day 13 to Day 14:** Continue your stay in Krakow. (Krakow visit count: 4)
16. **Day 14 to Day 15:** Continue your stay in Krakow. (Krakow visit count: 5)
17. **Day 15 to Day 16:** Spend your final day in Krakow. By the end of Day 16, you will have completed your 6-day stay in Krakow and your 16-day European trip. (Krakow visit count: 6)

------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 10 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You want to spend 3 days in Frankfurt. You want to spend 3 days in Bucharest. You would like to visit Stuttgart for 6 days. You have to attend a workshop in Stuttgart between day 5 and day 10.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Bucharest and Frankfurt, Frankfurt and Stuttgart.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 10 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
